{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0159R7yVHF1rmdRT9hqzjzP7","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator – System Architecture  \n     3→_Author: Core Platform Team_  \n     4→_Last updated: 2024-05-30_\n     5→\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→## 1  Overview\n     9→\n    10→LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator (repo root: `ml_nlp/lexilearn_orchestrator`) is a **modular, C-based, MVC platform** for large-scale, adaptive language-learning applications.  \n    11→The orchestrator stitches together Machine-Learning Ops essentials—hyper-parameter tuning, model registry, automated retraining—behind a clean Controller façade exposing a single public header `lexilearn.h`.\n    12→\n    13→Key design drivers:\n    14→\n    15→* **High throughput** ingestion of multimodal classroom data.\n    16→* **Pluggable NLP/ML strategies** switchable at run-time.\n    17→* **Deterministic** builds, reproducible experiments, and strong type-safety.\n    18→* **Embeddability** in web servers, desktop LMS plug-ins, and edge devices.\n    19→\n    20→---\n    21→\n    22→## 2  Top-Level Component Diagram\n    23→\n    24→```text\n    25→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n    26→│                            Application Layer                           │\n    27→│ ┌────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐                 │\n    28→│ │  REST API  │    │ CLI Pipeline │    │  Batch Jobs  │ <─┐             │\n    29→│ └────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘   │ Application │\n    30→└──────│───────────────────│───────────────────│───────────┘             │\n    31→       ▼                   ▼                   ▼                         ▼\n    32→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n    33→│                           MVC Orchestrator                             │\n    34→│ ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐                   │\n    35→│ │  View    │◀──────▶│ Controller│◀──────▶│  Model   │                   │\n    36→│ └──────────┘        └──────────┘        └──────────┘                   │\n    37→│  │                       ▲                   │                         │\n    38→│  │ Observer Pattern      │ Factory Pattern   │ Strategy Pattern        │\n    39→│  ▼                       │                   ▼                         │\n    40→│ ┌──────────┐    emits ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐             │\n    41→│ │ DriftMon │──────────▶  JobScheduler│────▶ ModelRegistry│             │\n    42→│ └──────────┘          └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘             │\n    43→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n    44→```\n    45→\n    46→Legend:\n    47→\n    48→* **Controller** – Pipeline orchestrator (`controller/*.c`).\n    49→* **Model** – NLP/ML pipeline (`model/*.c`), pluggable via Strategy Pattern.\n    50→* **View** – Dashboards & WebSocket push (`view/*.c`).\n    51→* **Feature Store** – External component accessed via gRPC (`external/featstore`).\n    52→\n    53→---\n    54→\n    55→## 3  Directory Layout\n    56→\n    57→```text\n    58→lexilearn_orchestrator/\n    59→├── include/\n    60→│   ├── lexilearn.h         # Umbrella public header\n    61→│   ├── controller.h        # Controller API\n    62→│   ├── model.h             # Model Strategy API\n    63→│   └── view.h              # Visualization API\n    64→├── src/\n    65→│   ├── controller/\n    66→│   ├── model/\n    67→│   ├── view/\n    68→│   ├── util/\n    69→│   └── main.c              # Embeddable entry\n    70→├── conf/\n    71→│   └── orchestrator.toml   # Build-time & run-time parameters\n    72→└── docs/\n    73→    └── architecture.md\n    74→```\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## 4  Key Design Patterns\n    79→\n    80→| Concern              | Pattern            | Implementation Notes                                     |\n    81→|----------------------|--------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|\n    82→| Pipeline management  | Pipeline Pattern   | `controller/pipeline.c` composes discrete stages.        |\n    83→| Model hot-swapping   | Strategy Pattern   | `model/strategy_*.c` registered in a function pointer v-table. |\n    84→| Training job factory | Factory Pattern    | `controller/job_factory.c` builds diverse training jobs. |\n    85→| Experiment tracking  | Model Registry     | `model/registry.c` (backed by SQLite + JSONB).           |\n    86→| Drift detection       | Observer Pattern  | `util/drift_monitor.c` emits `EVENT_DRIFT_*`.            |\n    87→\n    88→---\n    89→\n    90→## 5  Runtime Flow\n    91→\n    92→```mermaid\n    93→sequenceDiagram\n    94→    participant LMS\n    95→    participant Controller\n    96→    participant Model\n    97→    participant Registry\n    98→    participant View\n    99→\n   100→    LMS->>Controller: ingest_snapshot()\n   101→    Controller->>Model: preprocess()\n   102→    Model->>Model: train() / predict()\n   103→    Model-->>Controller: metrics, model_id\n   104→    Controller->>Registry: log_experiment()\n   105→    alt Drift detected\n   106→        Controller->>Controller: schedule_retraining()\n   107→    end\n   108→    Controller-->>View: push_metrics_ws()\n   109→```\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## 6  Concurrency & Memory Safety\n   114→\n   115→1. **Actor Model Threads**  \n   116→   Controller spins lightweight actors (`pthread`) pinned to CPU cores:\n   117→   * `actor_ingest`\n   118→   * `actor_train`\n   119→   * `actor_drift`\n   120→   * `actor_view`\n   121→\n   122→2. **Zero-Copy Buffers**  \n   123→   Multimodal records reside in lock-free, ring buffers (`util/ringbuf.c`).\n   124→\n   125→3. **Ownership Annotation**  \n   126→   Every public API symbol suffixed with `_ref` uses **Reference-Counted Handles**.\n   127→\n   128→Example:\n   129→\n   130→```c\n   131→// src/model/model.c\n   132→ml_model_ref\n   133→ml_model_create(const char *strategy_name, const ml_hparam_set *hparams) {\n   134→    ml_model_impl *impl = strategy_vtable_get(strategy_name);\n   135→    if (!impl) { return NULL; }\n   136→    ml_model_ref ref = RC_ALLOC(sizeof(struct ml_model));\n   137→    ref->impl = impl;\n   138→    ref->impl->init(ref, hparams);\n   139→    return ref;\n   140→}\n   141→```\n   142→\n   143→---\n   144→\n   145→## 7  Error Handling Philosophy\n   146→\n   147→* **Fail-Fast** in development, **graceful degradation** in production.\n   148→* Rich machine-parsable error structs:\n   149→\n   150→```c\n   151→typedef struct {\n   152→    ll_err_code code;        /* Enumerated error code */\n   153→    char        msg[256];    /* Human-readable context */\n   154→    char        trace_id[64];/* Correlation ID for logs & metrics */\n   155→} ll_error_t;\n   156→```\n   157→\n   158→* Controller propagates root-cause via Observer Pattern (`EVENT_ERROR`),\n   159→  surfaced as HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n   160→\n   161→---\n   162→\n   163→## 8  Extending the Model Layer\n   164→\n   165→To add a custom summarizer:\n   166→\n   167→1. Implement `model/strategy_summarizer.c`, exporting:\n   168→   ```c\n   169→   const ml_strategy_vtable *lexi_summarizer_strategy(void);\n   170→   ```\n   171→2. Register the strategy in `model/strategy_registry.c`:\n   172→   ```c\n   173→   REGISTER_STRATEGY(\"lexi_summarizer\", lexi_summarizer_strategy);\n   174→   ```\n   175→3. Specify in `orchestrator.toml`:\n   176→   ```toml\n   177→   [strategy]\n   178→   active = \"lexi_summarizer\"\n   179→   ```\n   180→Strategy picked up at run-time—no Controller code touched.\n   181→\n   182→---\n   183→\n   184→## 9  Build & Deployment\n   185→\n   186→```sh\n   187→$ mkdir build && cd build\n   188→$ cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\n   189→$ make -j$(nproc)\n   190→$ ctest        # unit + integration tests\n   191→$ ninja docs   # regenerate this file from sources\n   192→```\n   193→\n   194→CI pipeline (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) enforces:\n   195→\n   196→* Clang-Tidy, MISRA-C subset.\n   197→* Code coverage ≥ 85 %.\n   198→* Static analysis (`cppcheck`, `infer`).\n   199→* Containerized regression tests using Docker Compose (PostgreSQL + MinIO).\n   200→\n   201→---\n   202→\n   203→## 10  MLOps Integration\n   204→\n   205→* **Hyper-Parameter Sweep** – `controller/hparam_tuner.c` interfaces with Optuna via gRPC.\n   206→* **Model Registry** – Lightweight SQLite + S3 artifacts, versioned by semantic hash.\n   207→* **Drift Monitoring** – KL divergence threshold triggers retraining jobs.\n   208→* **Automated Retraining** – Controller pushes jobs onto RabbitMQ queue; workers auto-scale via K8s HPA.\n   209→* **Audit Trail** – `model/registry.c` persists lineage: dataset → commit SHA → metrics.\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## 11  Security Considerations\n   214→\n   215→* Data at rest encrypted using libsodium XChaCha20-Poly1305.\n   216→* TLS 1.3 enforced for gRPC and WebSocket endpoints.\n   217→* Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) integrated with LMS OAuth2 scopes.\n   218→* Secrets managed via HashiCorp Vault sidecar injector.\n   219→\n   220→---\n   221→\n   222→## 12  Future Roadmap\n   223→\n   224→| Version | Planned Feature                               | Status |\n   225→|---------|-----------------------------------------------|--------|\n   226→| v2.1    | Federated Learning across districts           | 🟡 PoC |\n   227→| v2.2    | On-device speech assessment (Edge TPU)        | ⬜ Backlog |\n   228→| v3.0    | Rust FFI for safety-critical routines         | ⬜ Backlog |\n   229→\n   230→---\n   231→\n   232→## 13  Glossary\n   233→\n   234→* **Feature Store** – Centralized service storing engineered features for offline & online use.\n   235→* **Model Registry** – Database tracking model versions, metrics, and provenance.\n   236→* **Strategy Pattern** – Encapsulates interchangeable algorithms behind a stable API.\n   237→* **Observer Pattern** – Event dispatcher decoupling producers & consumers.\n   238→\n   239→---\n   240→\n   241→© 2024 LexiLearn Inc. – Licensed under the Apache 2.0 License\n   242→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. 75→┌───────────────────────────────┐\n    76→│           View Layer          │\n    77→│  ───────────────────────────  │\n    78→│  * HTTP/WS Dashboard          │\n    79→│  * Progress Heat-maps         │\n    80→│  * Explainability Widgets     │\n    81→└───────────────▲───────────────┘\n    82→                │\n    83→ Observer Hooks │  (*Publisher/Subscriber pattern)  \n    84→                │\n    85→┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n    86→│         Controller Layer      │\n    87→│  ───────────────────────────  │\n    88→│  * LMS Ingestion Pipeline     │\n    89→│  * Experiment (*Pluggable algorithms)  \n    95→                │\n    96→┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n    97→│           Model Layer         │\n    98→│  ───────────────────────────  │\n    99→│  * 104→```\n   105→\n   106→*All layers are implemented in modern, modular C99 with strict static analysis and unit-test coverage.*\n   107→\n   108→---\n   109→\n   110→## 🛠️ Build Options\n   111→\n   112→| Make Target | Description                                         |\n   113→|-------------|-----------------------------------------------------|\n   114→| `make debug`| Build with `-g` symbols and AddressSanitizer        |\n   115→| `make test` | Compile & run unit tests via [µTest](https://github.com/silentbicycle/cmacro) |\n   116→| `make docs` | Generate API reference using Doxygen                |\n   117→| `make clean`| Remove artifacts                                    |\n   118→\n   119→Environment variables you may override:\n   120→\n   121→```bash\n   122→CC=clang              # default: gcc\n   123→CFLAGS=\"-O3 -march=native\"\n   124→PREFIX=/usr/local\n   125→```\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→## 🔌 Extending with New Algorithms\n   130→\n   131→All algorithms implement the `LexiModelStrategy` interface:\n   132→\n   133→```c\n   134→/* include/model_strategy.h */\n   135→typedef struct LexiModelStrategy {\n   136→    char     name[32];\n   137→    int    (*init)     (struct LexiModelStrategy *, const char *config);\n   138→    int    (*train)    (struct LexiModelStrategy *, const Dataset *);\n   139→    float  (*predict)  (struct LexiModelStrategy *, const Sample *);\n   140→    void   (*destroy)  (struct LexiModelStrategy *);\n   141→} LexiModelStrategy;\n   142→```\n   143→\n   144→Steps:\n   145→\n   146→1. Create `src/model/<my_algo>.c` and implement all callbacks.\n   147→2. Register the strategy in `model_registry.c`:\n   148→\n   149→```c\n   150→extern LexiModelStrategy g_my_algo_strategy;\n   151→registry_add_strategy(&g_my_algo_strategy);\n   152→```\n   153→\n   154→3. Rebuild (`make`) and reference `--model-type=\"my_algo\"` in CLI.\n   155→\n   156→---\n   157→\n   158→## 🧪 Testing\n   159→\n   160→```bash\n   161→# Run the full suite (unit + integration)\n   162→make test               \n   163→\n   164→# Filter by pattern\n   165→UTEST_FILTER=\"FeatureStore*\" make test\n   166→```\n   167→\n   168→Each pull request triggers CI (GitHub Actions) which runs:\n   169→\n   170→1. `make test`\n   171→2. `make docs`\n   172→3. `cppcheck` and `clang-tidy`\n   173→4. Coverage reporting via `gcovr`\n   174→\n   175→---\n   176→\n   177→## 📈 Model Monitoring & Retraining Workflow\n   178→\n   179→1. **Drift Detection** – The `drift_observer` monitors live inference stats against a baseline distribution.\n   180→2. **Notification** – When `p-value < 0.05`, an Observer event persists to the Model Registry.\n   181→3. **Scheduler Trigger** – The Controller’s cron engine spins up a new training job.\n   182→4. **Registry Version Bump** – Successful model artifacts are versioned (`v{major}.{minor}.{patch}`).\n   183→5. **A/B Deployment** – Canary release toggled via feature flag.\n   184→\n   185→Refer to `docs/mlops.md` for threshold tuning and Prometheus queries.\n   186→\n   187→---\n   188→\n   189→## 🖥️ Dashboard Preview\n   190→\n   191→![Dashboard Screenshot](docs/images/dashboard_preview.png)\n   192→\n   193→---\n   194→\n   195→## 📜 License\n   196→\n   197→```\n   198→Apache License 2.0\n   199→Copyright © 2024 ACME-Edu\n   200→```\n   201→\n   202→---\n   203→\n   204→## 🤝 Contributing\n   205→\n   206→1.     /* Hard timeout per training job      */\n   142→    bool        autoretrain_on_drift;   /* Auto-schedule retraining if drift  */\n   143→    uint32_t    drift_monitor_freq_min; /* Drift check cadence (minutes)      */\n   144→    uint32_t    _reserved;              /* Align to 8-byte boundary           */\n   145→} ll_pipeline_manager_cfg_t;\n   146→\n   147→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n   148→/*  Public API                                                               */\n   149→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n   150→\n   151→/**\n   152→ * @brief  Instantiate a Pipeline Manager.\n   153→ *\n   154→ * @param[in]  cfg      Pointer to caller-owned configuration.\n   155→ * @param[out] mgr_out  Location where the newly created handle is stored.\n   156→ *\n   157→ * @retval LL_CONTROLLER_SUCCESS   On success.\n   158→ * @retval LL_CONTROLLER_EINVAL    cfg == NULL || mgr_out == NULL.\n   159→ * @retval LL_CONTROLLER_EIO       Persistent storage/log dir is not writable.\n   160→ */\n   161→int ll_pipeline_manager_create(\n   162→        const ll_pipeline_manager_cfg_t  *cfg,\n   163→        ll_pipeline_manager_t           **mgr_out);\n   164→\n   165→/**\n   166→ * @brief Destroy a Pipeline Manager and free its resources.\n   167→ *\n   168→ * Safe to call with NULL; PROJECT OVERVIEW\n\n**LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator** is a production-grade, C-based Machine Learning/NLP platform designed for adaptive language-learning applications. It implements a clean **Model-View-Controller (MVC) pattern** with strong emphasis on:\n- Modular, pluggable algorithms\n- High-throughput data ingestion\n- MLOps automation (hyper-parameter tuning, model registry, drift detection)\n- Real-time dashboards and explainability\n- Embeddability in web servers, LMS plugins, and edge devices\n\n**Version**: 0.9.3 (Controller: 1.2.0, Orchestrator: 1.3.0)\n\n---\n\n### 2. KEY DESIGN PATTERNS\n\n| Pattern | Implementation | Purpose |\n|---------|---|---|\n| **MVC** | Controller, Model, View layers | Clean separation of concerns |\n| **Strategy Pattern** | `model_strategy.h` + pluggable implementations | Hot-swappable algorithms (BERT, n-gram, hybrid) |\n| **Factory Pattern** | `job_factory.{c,h}` | Create diverse training jobs dynamically |\n| **Observer Pattern** | `observer.h` + event system | Decouple drift detection from retraining |\n| **Pipeline Pattern** | `pipeline_manager.{c,h}` + stages | Orchestrate ETL → Training → Deployment |\n| **Model Registry** | `model_registry.{c,h}` | Versioning, lifecycle management, lineage |\n\n---\n\n### 4. CORE COMPONENTS DEEP DIVE\n\n#### **4.1 ORCHESTRATOR (Controller Hub)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/controller/orchestrator.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: Central command center coordinating the entire MLOps pipeline.\n\n**Key Responsibilities**:\n- Periodic ingestion of LMS data\n- Hyper-parameter tuning & model training\n- Model registration in the registry\n- Automated retraining triggers\n- Broadcasting Observer events\n\n**Public API** (orchestrator.h):\n```c\n// Lifecycle\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_create(const ll_orchestrator_cfg_t *cfg, ll_logger_fn logger_cb, ll_orchestrator_t **out_handle);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_start(ll_orchestrator_t *handle);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_stop(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, uint32_t timeout_ms);\nvoid ll_orchestrator_destroy(ll_orchestrator_t *handle);\n\n// Registration APIs\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_register_ingestion_source(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_ingest_cb ingest_fn);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_register_training_factory(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_training_job_factory_cb factory_fn);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_register_drift_observer(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_drift_observer_cb observer_fn);\n\n// Manual operations\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_submit_manual_training(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_context_t *ingest_ctx);\n```\n\n**Configuration Structure** (ll_orchestrator_cfg_t):\n- `instance_name`: Human-readable identifier\n- `max_concurrent_jobs`: Job limit\n- `feature_store_uri`, `model_registry_uri`, `metrics_uri`: Backend endpoints\n- `drift_scan_interval_sec`: Drift detection cadence\n- `log_level`: Verbosity threshold\n- `user_ctx`: Opaque pointer for callbacks\n\n**Error Codes**:\n```c\nLL_STATUS_OK, LL_STATUS_EINVAL, LL_STATUS_ENOMEM, LL_STATUS_ESTATE, \nLL_STATUS_EINTERNAL, LL_STATUS_ETIMEOUT, LL_STATUS_ENOTFOUND, \nLL_STATUS_EALREADY, LL_STATUS_EIO\n```\n\n**Implementation** (orchestrator.c):\n- Singleton pattern with mutex-protected state\n- `scheduler_mainloop()`: Background thread handling time-based & drift-triggered retraining\n- `start_training_pipeline()`: Orchestrates full training cycle (ingest → preprocess → tune → train → register)\n- Signal handlers for graceful SIGINT/SIGTERM shutdown\n\n---\n\n#### **4.2 PIPELINE MANAGER (Stage Orchestration)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/controller/pipeline/pipeline_manager.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: Implements the Pipeline Pattern for ETL, training, and deployment stages.\n\n**Pipeline States**:\n```c\nLL_PIPELINE_STATE_UNINITIALIZED, INITIALIZING, IDLE, INGESTING, \nTRAINING, EVALUATING, DEPLOYING, MONITORING, PAUSED, SHUTTING_DOWN, \nTERMINATED, ERROR\n```\n\n**Public job\n- `autoretrain_on_drift`: Auto-retrain on statistical drift\n- `drift_monitor_freq_min`: Drift check cadence\n\n**Pipeline Stages**:\n- **Ingestion Stage** (`ingestion_stage.{c,h}`): Pulls classroom data from LMS\n- **Training Stage** (`training_stage.{c,h}`): Runs model training, hyperparameter tuning\n- **Deployment Stage** (`deployment_stage.{c,h}`): A/B testing, canary releases\n\n**Event Types** (Observer Pattern):\n```c\nLL_PIPELINE_EVENT_DATA_INGESTED, TRAINING_STARTED, TRAINING_COMPLETED,\nEVALUATION_COMPLETED, MODEL_VERSIONED, DRIFT_DETECTED, \nRETRAINING_SCHEDULED, PIPELINE_ERROR, TERMINATED\n```\n\n---\n\n#### **4.3 MODEL REGISTRY (Versioning & Lineage)**\n\n**Files**: char *dashboard_server_version(void);\n```\n\n**Configuration** (DashboardServerOptions):\n- `port`: TCP port (default 8080)\n- `enable_tls`: HTTPS/WSS support\n- `tls_cert_path`, `tls_key_path`:     // Set length\n    void        *user_ctx);           // User context\n// Returns HTTP status (200, 400, 500, etc.)\n```\n\n**Error Codes**:\n```c\nDB_ERR_OK, DB_ERR_INIT, DB_ERR_BIND, DB_ERR_TLS, DB_ERR_WEBSOCKET,\nDB_ERR_INVALID_ARG, DB_ERR_ROUTE_EXISTS, DB_ERR_NO_MEM, DB_ERR_INTERNAL\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. CONCURRENCY MODEL\n\n**Actor-Based Threading**:\n- `actor_ingest`: LMS data ingestion\n- `actor_train`: Model training jobs\n- `actor_drift`: Drift detection loop\n- `actor_view`: Dashboard event dispatch\n\n**Synchronization**:\n- Mutex-protected shared state (`pthread_mutex_t`)\n- Lock-free ring buffers for multimodal records\n- Reference-counted handles for memory safety\n\n---\n\n### 14. ERROR HANDLING\n\n**Philosophy**: Fail-fast in development, graceful degradation in production.\n\n**Error Struct** (ll_error_t):\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    ll_err_code code;           // Enumerated error code\n    char        msg[256];       // Human-readable context\n    char        trace_id[64];   // Correlation ID for logs\n} ll_error_t;\n```\n\n**Propagation**: Via Observer Pattern → HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n\n---\n\n### 15. KEY DESIGN PATTERNS\n\n| Pattern | Implementation | Purpose |\n|---------|---|---|\n| **MVC** | Controller, Model, View layers | Clean separation of concerns |\n| **Strategy Pattern** | `model_strategy.h` + pluggable implementations | Hot-swappable algorithms (BERT, n-gram, hybrid) |\n| **Factory Pattern** | `job_factory.{c,h}` | Create diverse training jobs dynamically |\n| **Observer Pattern** | `observer.h` + event system | Decouple drift detection from retraining |\n| **Pipeline Pattern** | `pipeline_manager.{c,h}` + stages | Orchestrate ETL → Training → Deployment |\n| **Model Registry** | `model_registry.{c,h}` | Versioning, lifecycle management, lineage |\n\n---\n\n### 4. CORE COMPONENTS DEEP DIVE\n\n#### **4.1 ORCHESTRATOR (Controller Hub)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/controller/orchestrator.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: Central command center coordinating the entire MLOps pipeline.\n\n**Key Responsibilities**:\n- Periodic ingestion of LMS data\n- Hyper-parameter tuning & model training\n- Model registration in the registry\n- Automated retraining triggers\n- Broadcasting Observer events\n\n**Public API** (orchestrator.h):\n```c\n// Lifecycle\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_create(const ll_orchestrator_cfg_t *cfg, ll_logger_fn logger_cb, ll_orchestrator_t **out_handle);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_start(ll_orchestrator_t *handle);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_stop(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, uint32_t timeout_ms);\nvoid ll_orchestrator_destroy(ll_orchestrator_t *handle);\n\n// Registration APIs\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_register_ingestion_source(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_ingest_cb ingest_fn);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_register_training_factory(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_training_job_factory_cb factory_fn);\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_register_drift_observer(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_drift_observer_cb observer_fn);\n\n// Manual operations\nll_status_t ll_orchestrator_submit_manual_training(ll_orchestrator_t *handle, ll_context_t *ingest_ctx);\n```\n\n**Configuration Structure** (ll_orchestrator_cfg_t):\n- `instance_name`: Human-readable identifier\n- `max_concurrent_jobs`: Job limit\n- `feature_store_uri`, `model_registry_uri`, `metrics_uri`: Backend endpoints\n- `drift_scan_interval_sec`: Drift detection cadence\n- `log_level`: Verbosity threshold\n- `user_ctx`: Opaque pointer for callbacks\n\n**Error Codes**:\n```c\nLL_STATUS_OK, LL_STATUS_EINVAL, LL_STATUS_ENOMEM, LL_STATUS_ESTATE, \nLL_STATUS_EINTERNAL, LL_STATUS_ETIMEOUT, LL_STATUS_ENOTFOUND, \nLL_STATUS_EALREADY, LL_STATUS_EIO\n```\n\n**Implementation** (orchestrator.c):\n- Singleton pattern with mutex-protected state\n- `scheduler_mainloop()`: Background thread handling time-based & drift-triggered retraining\n- `start_training_pipeline()`: Orchestrates full training cycle (ingest → preprocess → tune → train → register)\n- Signal handlers for graceful SIGINT/SIGTERM shutdown\n\n---\n\n#### **4.2 PIPELINE MANAGER (Stage Orchestration)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/controller/pipeline/pipeline_manager.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: Implements the Pipeline Pattern for ETL, training, and deployment stages.\n\n**Pipeline States**:\n```c\nLL_PIPELINE_STATE_UNINITIALIZED, INITIALIZING, IDLE, INGESTING, \nTRAINING, EVALUATING, DEPLOYING, MONITORING, PAUSED, SHUTTING_DOWN, \nTERMINATED, ERROR\n```\n\n**Public API**:\n```c\nint ll_pipeline_manager_create(const ll_pipeline_manager_cfg_t *cfg, ll_pipeline_manager_t **mgr_out);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_start(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_pause(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_resume(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_stop(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_trigger_retrain(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\nll_pipeline_state_t ll_pipeline_manager_get_state(const ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_register_observer(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr, ll_pipeline_event_cb cb, ll_observer_ctx_t ctx, ll_observer_handle_t *handle);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_unregister_observer(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr, ll_observer_handle_t handle);\nint ll_pipeline_manager_flush(ll_pipeline_manager_t *mgr);\n```\n\n**Configuration** (ll_pipeline_manager_cfg_t):\n- `log_dir`: Pipeline-level logs\n- `experiment_name`: MLflow/Registry experiment\n- `max_concurrency`: Concurrent job limit\n- `training_timeout_s`: Hard timeout per job\n- `autoretrain_on_drift`: Auto-retrain on statistical drift\n- `drift_monitor_freq_min`: Drift check cadence\n\n**Pipeline Stages**:\n- **Ingestion Stage** (`ingestion_stage.{c,h}`): Pulls classroom data from LMS\n- **Training Stage** (`training_stage.{c,h}`): Runs model training, hyperparameter tuning\n- **Deployment Stage** (`deployment_stage.{c,h}`): A/B testing, canary releases\n\n**Event Types** (Observer Pattern):\n```c\nLL_PIPELINE_EVENT_DATA_INGESTED, TRAINING_STARTED, TRAINING_COMPLETED,\nEVALUATION_COMPLETED, MODEL_VERSIONED, DRIFT_DETECTED, \nRETRAINING_SCHEDULED, PIPELINE_ERROR, TERMINATED\n```\n\n---\n\n#### **4.3 MODEL REGISTRY (Versioning & Lineage)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/model/ml_pipeline/model_registry.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: Central, thread-safe SQLite-backed registry tracking model lifecycle, metrics, and provenance.\n\n**Model Stages**:\n```c\nML_STAGE_EXPERIMENT (0)  → ML_STAGE_STAGING (1) → ML_STAGE_PRODUCTION (2) → ML_STAGE_ARCHIVED (3)\n```\n\n**Model Metadata Struct** (ml_model_record_t):\n```c\nstruct ml_model_record_t {\n    char model_id[128];                 // PK: \"{strategy}::{git-sha}\"\n    char strategy[64];                  // Strategy pattern key\n    char version_tag[32];               // \"v1.2.3\"\n    char artifact_path[4096];           // Binary/pickle path\n    char hyperparams_json[8192];        // Training parameters\n    char metrics_json[8192];            // Evaluation metrics\n    char git_commit_sha[16];            // Code lineage\n    ml_model_stage_t stage;             // Current stage\n    time_t created_at;                  // Registration timestamp\n    time_t last_updated;                // Stage change timestamp\n}\n```\n\n**Public API**:\n```c\n// Lifecycle\nml_error_t ml_registry_init(const char *registry_root);\nml_error_t ml_registry_shutdown(void);\n\n// CRUD: Create\nml_error_t ml_registry_register_model(const char *strategy_name, const char *version_tag,\n                                      const char *artifact_path, const char *hyperparams_json,\n                                      const char *metrics_json, const char *git_commit_sha,\n                                      char *out_model_id, size_t out_model_id_len);\n\n// CRUD: Read\nml_error_t ml_registry_get_model(const char *model_id, ml_model_record_t *out_record);\nml_error_t ml_registry_get_latest_model(const char *strategy_name, ml_model_stage_t desired_stage,\n                                       char *out_model_id, size_t len);\nml_error_t ml_registry_list_models(const char *strategy_name, ml_model_record_t **out_list, size_t *out_count);\nvoid ml_registry_free_model_list(ml_model_record_t *list);\n\n// CRUD: Update\nml_error_t ml_registry_set_stage(const char *model_id, ml_model_stage_t new_stage);\nml_error_t ml_registry_update_metrics(const char *model_id, const char *metrics_json);\n\n// Delete / GC\nml_error_t ml_registry_archive_model(const char *model_id, int hard_delete);\n\n// Observability\nFILE *ml_registry_watch(void);\nvoid ml_registry_unwatch(FILE *stream);\n```\n\n**Error Codes**:\n```c\nML_OK, ML_ERR_INIT, SHUTDOWN, IO, MEMORY, INVALID_ARG, NOT_FOUND, \nCONFLICT, SERIALIZATION, UNKNOWN\n```\n\n**Thread Safety**: Optional compile-time feature (`ML_REGISTRY_THREAD_SAFE`)\n\n---\n\n#### **4.4 MODEL MONITOR (Drift Detection & Retraining)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/controller/monitoring/model_monitor.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: Real-time monitoring of model performance, drift detection, and automated retraining triggers.\n\n**Key Metrics Tracked** (mm_metrics_t):\n```c\nstruct mm_metrics_t {\n    double accuracy, f1_score, precision, recall;\n    double latency_ms, throughput_qps;\n    double data_drift;      // e.g., Population Stability Index (PSI)\n    double concept_drift;   // Sudden change detection score\n    time_t snapshot_time_utc;\n}\n```\n\n**Public API**:\n```c\n// Lifecycle\nmm_status_t mm_init(void);\nvoid mm_shutdown(void);\n\n// Model Registration\nmm_status_t mm_model_register(const char *model_id, const mm_metrics_t *baseline, mm_model_handle_t **handle_out);\nmm_status_t mm_model_unregister(mm_model_handle_t *handle);\n\n// Metrics Submission\nmm_status_t mm_submit_metrics(mm_model_handle_t *handle, const mm_metrics_t *metrics);\n\n// Observer Pattern\nmm_status_t mm_add_observer(mm_model_handle_t *handle, mm_observer_fn fn, void *user_data);\nmm_status_t mm_remove_observer(mm_model_handle_t *handle, mm_observer_fn fn, void *user_data);\n\n// Drift Detection Strategy (pluggable)\nmm_status_t mm_register_drift_detector(const char *name, const mm_drift_detector_vtbl_t *vtbl);\n\n// Imperative Retraining\nmm_status_t mm_force_retrain(mm_model_handle_t *handle, const char *reason);\n```\n\n**Event Types**:\n```c\nMM_EVENT_METRIC_UPDATE, MM_EVENT_DRIFT_DETECTED, MM_EVENT_RETRAIN_TRIGGERED, MM_EVENT_ERROR\n```\n\n**Drift Detector Strategy Interface** (mm_drift_detector_vtbl_t):\n```c\nstruct mm_drift_detector_vtbl_t {\n    mm_status_t (*init)(mm_model_handle_t *model, const mm_metrics_t *baseline);\n    mm_status_t (*update)(const mm_metrics_t *current_metrics, bool *drift_detected);\n    const char *(*diagnostics)(void);\n    void (*destroy)(void);\n}\n```\n\n**Implementations** (pluggable):\n- KL-divergence threshold-based detector\n- Custom statistical tests (PSI, KS-test)\n\n---\n\n#### **4.5 DASHBOARD SERVER (View Layer)**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/view/dashboard_server.{c,h}`\n\n**Purpose**: HTTP/WebSocket server for real-time dashboards, metrics streaming, and educator control.\n\n**Features**:\n- HTTP REST endpoints for manual interventions (e.g., `/api/retrain`)\n- WebSocket channels for real-time metric push\n- Binary artifact broadcasting (heat-maps, explainability plots)\n- TLS/HTTPS support\n- CORS support\n- Client connection limits\n\n**Public API**:\n```c\n// Lifecycle\nDashboardServer *dashboard_server_create(const DashboardServerOptions *opts);\ndb_err_t dashboard_server_start(DashboardServer *srv);\nvoid dashboard_server_stop(DashboardServer *srv);\nvoid dashboard_server_destroy(DashboardServer *srv);\n\n// Route Registration\ndb_err_t dashboard_server_register_route(DashboardServer *srv, const char *method,\n                                        const char *route, dashboard_route_cb cb, void *user_ctx);\n\n// Publishing\ndb_err_t dashboard_server_publish(DashboardServer *srv, const char *channel_name, const char *json_payload);\ndb_err_t dashboard_server_broadcast(DashboardServer *srv, const void *data, size_t data_len, const char *mime_type);\n\n// Utilities\ndb_err_t dashboard_server_block_until_signal(DashboardServer *srv);\nconst char *dashboard_server_version(void);\n```\n\n**Configuration** (DashboardServerOptions):\n- `port`: TCP port (default 8080)\n- `enable_tls`: HTTPS/WSS support\n- `tls_cert_path`, `tls_key_path`: PEM-encoded credentials\n- `static_asset_dir`: HTML/JS document root\n- `max_clients`: Hard connection limit\n- `enable_cors`: CORS headers\n\n**Route Callback**:\n```c\ntypedef int (*dashboard_route_cb)(\n    const char  *uri,\n    const char  *request_body,\n    size_t       request_len,\n    char       **response_body,       // Allocate & set\n    size_t      *response_len,        // Set length\n    void        *user_ctx);           // User context\n// Returns HTTP status (200, 400, 500, etc.)\n```\n\n**Error Codes**:\n```c\nDB_ERR_OK, DB_ERR_INIT, DB_ERR_BIND, DB_ERR_TLS, DB_ERR_WEBSOCKET,\nDB_ERR_INVALID_ARG, DB_ERR_ROUTE_EXISTS, DB_ERR_NO_MEM, DB_ERR_INTERNAL\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. MODEL LAYER (Strategy Pattern)\n\n#### **5.1 Model Strategy Interface**\n\n**Files**: `/app/project/src/model/ml_models/model_strategy.h`\n\n**Purpose**: Formalizes the Strategy Pattern contract for model_strategy_vtable_t *);\n}\n```\n\nAllows educators to swap algorithms without recompiling the Controller.\n\n#### **5.2 Concrete Model Implementations**\n\n| File | Purpose BUILD SYSTEM\n\n**Build Tool**: CMake 3.13+\n\n**Compilation**:\n```bash\nmkdir build && cd build\ncmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\nmake -j$(nproc)\nctest                    # Run tests\n```\n\n**CI/CD Pipeline** (.github/workflows/ci.yml):\n- Clang-Tidy + MISRA-C linting\n- Code coverage ≥ 85%\n- Static analysis (cppcheck, infer)\n- Docker Compose regression tests\n\n---\n\n### 12. RUNTIME FLOW (Sequence Diagram)\n\n```\nLMS → Controller: ingest_snapshot()\n      ↓\nController → Model: preprocess()\n      ↓\nModel → Model: train() / predict()\n      ↓\nModel → Controller: metrics, model_id\n      ↓\nController → Registry: log_experiment()\n      ↓\n[Drift Detection Loop]\nController → Controller: schedule_retraining() [if drift > threshold]\n      ↓\nController → View: push_metrics_ws()\n      ↓\nView → Dashboard: Real-time visualization\n```\n\n---\n\n### 13. CONCURRENCY MODEL\n\n**Actor-Based Threading**:\n- `actor_ingest`: LMS data ingestion\n- `actor_train`: Model training jobs\n- `actor_drift`: Drift detection loop\n- `actor_view`: Dashboard event dispatch\n\n**Synchronization**:\n- Mutex-protected shared state (`pthread_mutex_t`)\n- Lock-free ring buffers for multimodal records\n- Reference-counted handles for memory safety\n\n---\n\n### 14. ERROR HANDLING\n\n**Philosophy**: Fail-fast in development, graceful degradation in production.\n\n**Error Struct** (ll_error_t):\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    ll_err_code code;           // Enumerated error code\n    char        msg[256];       // Human-readable context\n    char        trace_id[64];   // Correlation ID for logs\n} ll_error_t;\n```\n\n**Propagation**: Via Observer Pattern → HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n\n---\n\n### 15. MLOps INTEGRATION\n\n| Feature | Component | Details |\n|---------|-----------|---------|\n| **Hyperparameter Tuning** | `hparam_tuner.c` | Optuna integration via gRPC |\n| **Model Registry** | `model_registry.{c,h}` | SQLite + S3 artifacts, semantic versioning |\n| **Drift Monitoring** | `drift_detector.{c,h}` | KL divergence, PSI, custom statistics |\n| **Automated Retraining** | `orchestrator.c` + `scheduler` | RabbitMQ job queue, K8s HPA scaling |\n| **Audit Trail** | `model_registry.c` | Lineage: dataset → commit SHA → metrics |\n\n---\n\n### 16. SECURITY\n\n**Data Protection**:\n- At-rest encryption: libsodium XChaCha20-Poly1305\n- In-transit: TLS 1.3 for gRPC & WebSocket\n- RBAC: Integrated with LMS OAuth2 scopes\n- Secrets: HashiCorp Vault sidecar injection\n\n---\n\n### 17. FILE COUNT & STATISTICS\n\n- **Total Source Files**: 57 (.c + .h files)\n- **Total Lines**: ~15,000+ lines of production C code\n- **Public Headers**: 4 (include/lexilearn/)\n- **Implementation Headers**: 18 (internal APIs)\n- **Data Structures**: 2 (vector, hashmap)\n- **Test Coverage**: ≥ 85% (enforced by CI)\n\n---\n\n### 18. EXTENSION POINTS\n\n**To Add a New Model Strategy**:\n1. Create `src/model/strategy_<name>.c`\n2. Implement `model_strategy_vtable_t` interface\n3. Register in strategy registry\n4. Reference in config file\n\n**To Add a New Drift Detector**:\n1. Implement `mm_drift_detector_vtbl_t`\n2. Call `mm_register_drift_detector()`\n\n**To Add a Custom Dashboard Route**:\n1. Architecture  \n     3→_Author: Core Platform Team_  \n     4→_Last updated: 2024-05-30_\n     5→\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→## 1  Overview\n     9→\n    10→LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator (repo root: `ml_nlp/lexilearn_orchestrator`) is a **modular, C-based, MVC platform** for large-scale, adaptive language-learning applications.  \n    11→The orchestrator stitches together Machine-Learning Ops essentials—hyper-parameter tuning, model registry, automated retraining—behind a clean Controller façade exposing a single public header `lexilearn.h`.\n    12→\n    13→Key design drivers:\n    14→\n    15→* **High throughput** ingestion of multimodal classroom data.\n    16→* **Pluggable NLP/ML strategies** switchable at run-time.\n    17→* **Deterministic** builds, reproducible experiments, and strong type-safety.\n    18→* **Embeddability** in web servers, desktop LMS plug-ins, and edge devices.\n    19→\n    20→---\n    21→\n    22→## 2  Top-Level Component Diagram\n    23→\n Concern              | Pattern            | Implementation Notes                                     |\n    81→|----------------------|--------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|\n    82→| Pipeline management  | Pipeline Pattern   | `controller/pipeline.c` composes discrete stages.        |\n    83→| Model hot-swapping   | Strategy Pattern   | `model/strategy_*.c` registered in a function pointer v-table. |\n    84→| Training job factory | Factory Pattern    | `controller/job_factory.c` builds diverse training jobs. |\n    85→| Experiment tracking  | Model Registry     | `model/registry.c` (backed by SQLite + JSONB).           |\n    86→| Drift detection       | Observer Pattern  | `util/drift_monitor.c` emits `EVENT_DRIFT_*`.            |\n    87→\n    88→---\n    89→\n    90→## 5  Runtime Flow\n    91→\n    92→```mermaid\n    93→sequenceDiagram\n ingest_snapshot()\n   101→    Controller->>Model: preprocess()\n   102→    Model->>Model: train() / predict()\n   103→    Model-->>Controller: metrics, model_id\n   104→    Controller->>Registry: log_experiment()\n   105→    alt Drift detected\n   106→        Controller->>Controller: schedule_retraining()\n   107→    end\n   108→    Controller-->>View: push_metrics_ws()\n   109→```\n   110→\n `actor_ingest`\n   118→   * `actor_train`\n   119→   * `actor_drift`\n   120→   * `actor_view`\n   121→\n   122→2. **Zero-Copy Buffers**  \n   123→   Multimodal records reside in lock-free, ring buffers (`util/ringbuf.c`).\n   124→\n   125→3. **Ownership Annotation**  \n   126→   Every public API symbol suffixed with `_ref` uses **Reference-Counted Handles**.\n   127→\n   128→Example:\n   129→\n   130→```c\n   131→// src/model/model.c\n   132→ml_model_ref\n   133→ml_model_create(const char *strategy_name, const ml_hparam_set *hparams) {\n   134→    ml_model_impl *impl = ref->impl->init(ref, hparams);\n   139→    return ref;\n   140→}\n   141→```\n   142→\n   143→---\n   144→\n   145→## 7  Error Handling Philosophy\n   146→\n   147→* **Fail-Fast** in development, **graceful degradation** in production.\n   148→* Rich machine-parsable error structs:\n   149→\n   150→```c\n   151→typedef struct {\n   152→    ll_err_code code;        /* Enumerated error code */\n   153→    char        msg[256];    /* Human-readable context */\n   154→    char        trace_id[64];/* Correlation ID for logs & metrics */\n   155→} ll_error_t;\n   156→```\n   157→\n   158→* Controller propagates root-cause via Observer Pattern (`EVENT_ERROR`),\n   159→  surfaced as HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n   160→\n   161→---\n   162→\n   163→## 8  Extending the Model Layer\n   164→\n   165→To add a custom summarizer:\n   166→\n   167→1. Implement `model/strategy_summarizer.c`, exporting:\n   168→   ```c\n   169→   const ml_strategy_vtable *lexi_summarizer_strategy(void);\n   170→   ```\n   171→2. Register the strategy in `model/strategy_registry.c`:\n   172→   ```c\n   173→   REGISTER_STRATEGY(\"lexi_summarizer\", lexi_summarizer_strategy);\n   174→   ```\n   175→3. Specify in `orchestrator.toml`:\n   176→   ```toml\n   177→   [strategy]\n   178→   active = \"lexi_summarizer\"\n   179→   ```\n   180→Strategy picked up at run-time—no Controller code touched.\n   181→\n   182→---\n   183→\n   184→## 9  Build & Deployment\n   185→\n   186→```sh\n   187→$ mkdir build && cd build\n   188→$ cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\n   189→$ make -j$(nproc)\n   190→$ ctest        # unit + integration tests\n   191→$ ninja docs   # regenerate this file from sources\n   192→```\n   193→\n   194→CI pipeline (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) enforces:\n   195→\n   196→* Clang-Tidy, MISRA-C subset.\n   197→* Code coverage ≥ 85 %.\n   198→* Static analysis (`cppcheck`, `infer`).\n   199→* Containerized regression tests using Docker Compose (PostgreSQL + MinIO).\n   200→\n   201→---\n   202→\n   203→## 10  MLOps Integration\n   204→\n   205→* **Hyper-Parameter Sweep** – `controller/hparam_tuner.c` interfaces with Optuna via gRPC.\n   206→* **Model Registry** – Lightweight SQLite + S3 artifacts, versioned by semantic hash.\n   207→* **Drift Monitoring** – KL divergence threshold triggers retraining jobs.\n   208→* **Automated Retraining** – Controller pushes jobs onto RabbitMQ queue; workers auto-scale via K8s HPA.\n   209→* **Audit Trail** – `model/registry.c` persists lineage: dataset → commit SHA → metrics.\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## 11  Security Considerations\n   214→\n   215→* Data at rest encrypted using libsodium XChaCha20-Poly1305.\n   216→* TLS 1.3 enforced for gRPC and WebSocket endpoints.\n   217→* Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) integrated with LMS OAuth2 scopes.\n   218→* Secrets managed via HashiCorp Vault sidecar injector.\n   219→\n   220→---\n   221→\n   222→## 12  Future Roadmap\n   223→\n   224→| Version | Planned Feature                               | Status |\n   225→|---------|-----------------------------------------------|--------|\n   226→| v2.1    | Federated Learning across districts           | 🟡 PoC |\n   227→| v2.2    | On-device speech assessment (Edge TPU)        | ⬜ Backlog |\n   228→| v3.0    | Rust FFI for safety-critical routines         | ⬜ Backlog |\n   229→\n   230→---\n   231→\n   232→## 13  Glossary\n   233→\n   234→* **Feature Store** – Centralized service storing engineered features for offline & online use.\n   235→* **Model Registry** – Database tracking model versions, metrics, and provenance.\n   236→* **Strategy Pattern** – Encapsulates interchangeable algorithms behind a stable API.\n   237→* **Observer Pattern** – Event dispatcher decoupling producers & consumers.\n   238→\n   239→---\n   240→\n   241→© 2024 LexiLearn Inc. – Licensed under the Apache 2.0 License\n   242→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. LexiLearn MVC Orchestrator – System Architecture  \n_Author: Core Platform Team_  \n_Last updated: 2024-05-30_\n\n---\n\n## 1  Overview\n\nLexiLearn MVC Orchestrator (repo root: `ml_nlp/lexilearn_orchestrator`) is a **modular, C-based, MVC platform** for large-scale, adaptive language-learning applications.  \nThe orchestrator stitches together Machine-Learning Ops essentials—hyper-parameter tuning, model registry, automated retraining—behind a clean Controller façade exposing a single public header `lexilearn.h`.\n\nKey design drivers:\n\n* **High throughput** ingestion of multimodal classroom data.\n* **Pluggable NLP/ML strategies** switchable at run-time.\n* **Deterministic** builds, reproducible experiments, and strong type-safety.\n* **Embeddability** in web servers, desktop LMS plug-ins, and edge devices.\n\n---\n\n## 2  Top-Level Component Diagram\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                            Application Layer                           │\n│ ┌────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐                 │\n│ │  REST API  │    │ CLI Pipeline │    │  Batch Jobs  │ <─┐             │\n│ └────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘   │ Application │\n└──────│───────────────────│───────────────────│───────────┘             │\n       ▼                   ▼                   ▼                         ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                           MVC Orchestrator                             │\n│ ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐  uses  ┌──────────┐                   │\n│ │  View    │◀──────▶│ Controller│◀──────▶│  Model   │                   │\n│ └──────────┘        └──────────┘        └──────────┘                   │\n│  │                       ▲                   │                         │\n│  │ Observer Pattern      │ Factory Pattern   │ Strategy Pattern        │\n│  ▼                       │                   ▼                         │\n│ ┌──────────┐    emits ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐             │\n│ │ DriftMon │──────────▶  JobScheduler│────▶ ModelRegistry│             │\n│ └──────────┘          └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nLegend:\n\n* **Controller** – Pipeline orchestrator (`controller/*.c`).\n* **Model** – NLP/ML pipeline (`model/*.c`), pluggable via Strategy Pattern.\n* **View** – Dashboards & WebSocket push (`view/*.c`).\n* **Feature Store** – External component accessed via gRPC (`external/featstore`).\n\n---\n\n## 3  Directory Layout\n\n```text\nlexilearn_orchestrator/\n├── include/\n│   ├── lexilearn.h         # Umbrella public header\n│   ├── controller.h        # Controller API\n│   ├── model.h             # Model Strategy API\n│   └── view.h              # Visualization API\n├── src/\n│   ├── controller/\n│   ├── model/\n│   ├── view/\n│   ├── util/\n│   └── main.c              # Embeddable entry\n├── conf/\n│   └── orchestrator.toml   # Build-time & run-time parameters\n└── docs/\n    └── architecture.md\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4  Key Design Patterns\n\n| Concern              | Pattern            | Implementation Notes                                     |\n|----------------------|--------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|\n| Pipeline management  | Pipeline Pattern   | `controller/pipeline.c` composes discrete stages.        |\n| Model hot-swapping   | Strategy Pattern   | `model/strategy_*.c` registered in a function pointer v-table. |\n| Training job factory | Factory Pattern    | `controller/job_factory.c` builds diverse training jobs. |\n| Experiment tracking  | Model Registry     | `model/registry.c` (backed by SQLite + JSONB).           |\n| Drift detection       | Observer Pattern  | `util/drift_monitor.c` emits `EVENT_DRIFT_*`.            |\n\n---\n\n## 5  Runtime Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant LMS\n    participant Controller\n    participant Model\n    participant Registry\n    participant View\n\n    LMS->>Controller: ingest_snapshot()\n    Controller->>Model: preprocess()\n    Model->>Model: train() / predict()\n    Model-->>Controller: metrics, model_id\n    Controller->>Registry: log_experiment()\n    alt Drift detected\n        Controller->>Controller: schedule_retraining()\n    end\n    Controller-->>View: push_metrics_ws()\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6  Concurrency & Memory Safety\n\n1. **Actor Model Threads**  \n   Controller spins lightweight actors (`pthread`) pinned to CPU cores:\n   * `actor_ingest`\n   * `actor_train`\n   * `actor_drift`\n   * `actor_view`\n\n2. **Zero-Copy Buffers**  \n   Multimodal records reside in lock-free, ring buffers (`util/ringbuf.c`).\n\n3. **Ownership Annotation**  \n   Every public API symbol suffixed with `_ref` uses **Reference-Counted Handles**.\n\nExample:\n\n```c\n// src/model/model.c\nml_model_ref\nml_model_create(const char *strategy_name, const ml_hparam_set *hparams) {\n    ml_model_impl *impl = strategy_vtable_get(strategy_name);\n    if (!impl) { return NULL; }\n    ml_model_ref ref = RC_ALLOC(sizeof(struct ml_model));\n    ref->impl = impl;\n    ref->impl->init(ref, hparams);\n    return ref;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7  Error Handling Philosophy\n\n* **Fail-Fast** in development, **graceful degradation** in production.\n* Rich machine-parsable error structs:\n\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    ll_err_code code;        /* Enumerated error code */\n    char        msg[256];    /* Human-readable context */\n    char        trace_id[64];/* Correlation ID for logs & metrics */\n} ll_error_t;\n```\n\n* Controller propagates root-cause via Observer Pattern (`EVENT_ERROR`),\n  surfaced as HTTP 5xx or WebSocket messages in View.\n\n---\n\n## 8  Extending the Model Layer\n\nTo add a custom summarizer:\n\n1. Implement `model/strategy_summarizer.c`, exporting:\n   ```c\n   const ml_strategy_vtable *lexi_summarizer_strategy(void);\n   ```\n2. Register the strategy in `model/strategy_registry.c`:\n   ```c\n   REGISTER_STRATEGY(\"lexi_summarizer\", lexi_summarizer_strategy);\n   ```\n3. Specify in `orchestrator.toml`:\n   ```toml\n   [strategy]\n   active = \"lexi_summarizer\"\n   ```\nStrategy picked up at run-time—no Controller code touched.\n\n---\n\n## 9  Build & Deployment\n\n```sh\n$ mkdir build && cd build\n$ cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\n$ make -j$(nproc)\n$ ctest        # unit + integration tests\n$ ninja docs   # regenerate this file from sources\n```\n\nCI pipeline (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) enforces:\n\n* Clang-Tidy, MISRA-C subset.\n* Code coverage ≥ 85 %.\n* Static analysis (`cppcheck`, `infer`).\n* Containerized regression tests using Docker Compose (PostgreSQL + MinIO).\n\n---\n\n## 10  MLOps Integration\n\n* **Hyper-Parameter Sweep** – `controller/hparam_tuner.c` interfaces with Optuna via gRPC.\n* **Model Registry** – Lightweight SQLite + S3 artifacts, versioned by semantic hash.\n* **Drift Monitoring** – KL divergence threshold triggers retraining jobs.\n* **Automated Retraining** – Controller pushes jobs onto RabbitMQ queue; workers auto-scale via K8s HPA.\n* **Audit Trail** – `model/registry.c` persists lineage: dataset → commit SHA → metrics.\n\n---\n\n## 11  Security Considerations\n\n* Data at rest encrypted using libsodium XChaCha20-Poly1305.\n* TLS 1.3 enforced for gRPC and WebSocket endpoints.\n* Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) integrated with LMS OAuth2 scopes.\n* Secrets managed via HashiCorp Vault sidecar injector.\n\n---\n\n## 12  Future Roadmap\n\n| Version | Planned Feature                               | Status |\n|---------|-----------------------------------------------|--------|\n| v2.1    | Federated Learning across districts           | 🟡 PoC |\n| v2.2    | On-device speech assessment (Edge TPU)        | ⬜ Backlog |\n| v3.0    | Rust FFI for safety-critical routines         | ⬜ Backlog |\n\n---\n\n## 13  Glossary\n\n* **Feature Store** – Centralized service storing engineered features for offline & online use.\n* **Model Registry** – Database tracking model versions, metrics, and provenance.\n* **Strategy Pattern** – Encapsulates interchangeable algorithms behind a stable API.\n* **Observer Pattern** – Event dispatcher decoupling producers & consumers.\n\n---\n\n© 2024 LexiLearn Inc. – Licensed under the Apache 2.0 License\n```","numLines":242,"startLine":1,"totalLines":242}}}

observable_subscribe(observable_t *observable,\n                                       observer_t   *subscriber)\n{\n    if (!observable || !subscriber || !subscriber->on_event)\n        return -1; /* observer_t   *subscriber)\n{\n    if (!observable || !subscriber)\n        return -1; /* invalid args */\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < observable->count; ++i)\n        if (observable->subscribers[i] sub->on_event)\n            sub->on_event(event, sub->user_data);\n    }\n}\n\n/* Event Factory ---------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic inline observer_event_t              metric_value,\n                                                     const char           *payload)\n{\n    observer_event_t ev;\n\n    ev.type   = type;\n    ev.id     = 1);\n        ev.model_name[OBSERVER_MAX_NAME_LEN - 1] = '\\0';\n    } else {\n        ev.model_name[0] = '\\0';\n    }\n\n    ev.metric_value = metric_value;\n    ev.payload      = payload;\n    return ev;\n}\n\n/* Stringify Event Type --------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic inline const char *observer_event_type_str(observer_event_type_t type)\n{\n    switch (type) {\n case EVENT_TRAINING_START:        return \"TRAINING_START\";\n        case EVENT_TRAINING_END:          return \"TRAINING_END\";\n        case EVENT_HPARAM_TUNING_START:   return      default:\n   360→            det->last_score = 0.0;\n   361→            break;\n   362→    }\n   363→\n   364→    bool drift_now = det->last_score >= det->cfg.threshold;\n   365→    if (drift_now                void*                  ctx)\n   416→{\n   417→    if (!det || !fn) return LEXI_EINVAL;\n   418→\n   419→    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n   420→    size_t i = 0;\n   421→    while (i det->listener_ctx[j];\n   430→            }\n   431→            det->listener_cnt--;\n   432→            /* do not increment i to catch duplicate matches */\n   433→        }\n   434→        else\n   435→        {\n   436→            ++i;\n   437→        }\n   438→    }\n   439→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n   440→    return LEXI_OK;\n   441→}\n   442→\n   443→#endif /* i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        double a = act[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : act[i];\n        double r = ref[i] <= 0.0 ? 1e-12 : ref[i];\n        kl += a * log(a / r);\n    }\n    return kl;\n}\n\nstatic double\nlexi_compute_wasserstein(const double* ref, const double* act, size_t k)\n{\n    /* 1-dimensional 1-Wasserstein distance for histograms */\n    double cum_ref = 0.0, cum_act = 0.0, wass = 0.0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        cum_ref += ref[i];\n        cum_act += act[i];\n        wass += fabs(cum_ref - cum_act);\n    }\n    return wass;\n}\n\n/* ====  Private helper: notify listeners  ================================== */\nstatic void\nlexi_notify(const lexi_drift_detector_t* det)\n{\n    lexi_drift_event_t evt;\n    strncpy(evt.model_id, det->cfg.model_id, LEXI_MODEL_ID_LEN);\n    evt.score     = det->last_score;\n    evt.threshold = det->cfg.threshold;\n    evt.timestamp = time(NULL);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < cfg->num_bins; ++i)\n        det->reference[i] = 1.0 / (double)cfg->num_bins;\n\n    if (pthread_mutex_init(&det->mtx, NULL) != 0)\n    {\n        free(det);\n        if (err) *err = LEXI_ETHREAD;\n        return NULL;\n    }\n    if (err) *err = LEXI_OK;\n    return det;\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: Destroy  ============================================== */\nvoid\nlexi_drift_destroy(lexi_drift_detector_t* det)\n{\n    if (!det) return;\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&det->mtx);\n    free(det);\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: Update  =============================================== */\nvoid\nlexi_drift_update(lexi_drift_detector_t* det,\n                  const double*          probs,\n                  lexi_error_t*          err)\n{\n    if (!det || !probs)\n    {\n        if (err) *err = LEXI_EINVAL;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n    const size_t k = det->cfg.num_bins;\n\n    /* Exponential moving average for rolling distribution */\n    const double alpha = 1.0 / (double)LEXI_MAX(1, det->cfg.window);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < k; ++i)\n    {\n        double p = probs[i];\n        if (p < 0.0)\n        {\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n            if (err) *err = LEXI_EINVAL;\n            return;\n        }\n        det->rolling_actual[i] =\n            (1.0 - alpha) * det->rolling_actual[i] + alpha * p;\n    }\n\n    /* Compute drift metric */\n    switch (det->cfg.metric)\n    {\n        case LEXI_DRIFT_PSI:\n            det->last_score = lexi_compute_psi(det->reference,\n                                               det->rolling_actual, k);\n            break;\n        case LEXI_DRIFT_KL:\n            det->last_score = lexi_compute_kl(det->reference,\n                                              det->rolling_actual, k);\n            break;\n        case LEXI_DRIFT_WASSER:\n            det->last_score = lexi_compute_wasserstein(det->reference,\n                                                       det->rolling_actual, k);\n            break;\n        default:\n            det->last_score = 0.0;\n            break;\n    }\n\n    bool drift_now = det->last_score >= det->cfg.threshold;\n    if (drift_now && !det->drifting)\n    {\n        det->drifting = true;\n        lexi_notify(det); /* fire Observer callbacks */\n    }\n    else if (!drift_now && det->drifting)\n    {\n        det->drifting = false; /* reset flag */\n    }\n\n    det->sample_count++;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n\n    if (err) *err = LEXI_OK;\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: is_drifting  ========================================== */\nbool\nlexi_drift_is_drifting(const lexi_drift_detector_t* det, double* out_score)\n{\n    if (!det) return false;\n    if (out_score) *out_score = det->last_score;\n    return det->drifting;\n}\n\n/* ====  Public API: add/remove listener  =================================== */\nlexi_error_t\nlexi_drift_add_listener(lexi_drift_detector_t* det,\n                        lexi_drift_listener_t  fn,\n                        void*                  ctx)\n{\n    if (!det || !fn) return LEXI_EINVAL;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n\n    if (det->listener_cnt >= LEXI_MAX_LISTENERS)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n        return LEXI_ELISTENER_FULL;\n    }\n    det->listeners[det->listener_cnt]     = fn;\n    det->listener_ctx[det->listener_cnt]  = ctx;\n    det->listener_cnt++;\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n    return LEXI_OK;\n}\n\nlexi_error_t\nlexi_drift_remove_listener(lexi_drift_detector_t* det,\n                           lexi_drift_listener_t  fn,\n                           void*                  ctx)\n{\n    if (!det || !fn) return LEXI_EINVAL;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&det->mtx);\n    size_t i = 0;\n    while (i < det->listener_cnt)\n    {\n        if (det->listeners[i] == fn && det->listener_ctx[i] == ctx)\n        {\n            /* shift elements */\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < det->listener_cnt; ++j)\n            {\n                det->listeners[j - 1]    = det->listeners[j];\n                det->listener_ctx[j - 1] = det->listener_ctx[j];\n            }\n            det->listener_cnt--;\n            /* do not increment i to catch duplicate matches */\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            ++i;\n        }\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&det->mtx);\n    return LEXI_OK;\n}\n\n#endif /* LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION */\n#endif /* LEXILEARN_DRIFT_DETECTOR_H */\n","numLines":445,"startLine":1,"totalLines":445}}}

ingestion context\n * @return           LL_STATUS_OK on success, else error code\n */\ntypedef ll_status_t (*ll_ingest_cb)(\n        void            *user_ctx,\n        ll_context_t   **out_ctx);\n\n/**\n * @brief Factory callback that creates a training job for a given dataset.\n *\n * The factory can inspect `ingest_ctx` to decide which Strategy implementation\n * (BERT Summarizer, n-gram analyzer, etc.) should be constructed.\n *\n * @param user_ctx      Same pointer provided in ll_orchestrator_cfg_t::user_ctx\n * @param ingest_ctx    Immutable context belonging to the ingestion batch\n * @param out_job       (OUT) Newly created training job handle\n * @return              status code\n */\ntypedef ll_status_t (*ll_training_job_factory_cb)(\n        void                   *user_ctx,\n        const ll_context_t     *ingest_ctx,\n        ll_training_job_t     **out_job);\n\n/**\n * @brief Observer invoked when statistical drift is detected.\n *\n * @param user_ctx       Passed through from cfg\n * @param model_version  Version string of the affected model\n * @param drift_score    Magnitude of detected drift (0 = no drift)\n * @param timestamp      Wall-clock time of detection\n */\ntypedef void (*ll_drift_observer_cb)(\n        void            *user_ctx,\n        const char      *model_version,\n        double           drift_score,\n        time_t           timestamp);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Public API                                                               */\n/* (OUT) Newly created orchestrator\n * @return              status code\n */\nll_status_t\nll_orchestrator_create(\n        const ll_orchestrator_cfg_t *cfg,\n        ll_logger_fn     uint32_t           timeout_ms);\n\n/**\n * @brief Release all resources associated with the orchestrator.\n *\n * You must call ll_orchestrator_stop() before destroy.\n Aborting startup.\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Spawn the retraining scheduler */\n    int rc = pthread_create(&g_orch.scheduler_thread, NULL, scheduler_mainloop, NULL);\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Failed to start scheduler thread (%s).\", strerror(rc));\n        g_orch.running = 0;\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Orchestrator started.\");\n    return 0;\n}\n\nvoid orchestrator_stop(void)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_orch.state_mtx);\n    if (!g_orch.running)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_orch.state_mtx);\n        return;\n    }\n    g_orch.running = 0;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_orch.state_mtx);\n\n    /* Wake the scheduler so it can exit */\n    scheduler_cancel_wait();\n\n    pthread_join(g_orch.scheduler_thread, NULL);\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Orchestrator stopped.\");\n}\n\n/* ───────────── Implementation Details ───────────── */\n\n/**\n * start_training_pipeline()\n * ----------------------------------------------------\n * Executes a full training cycle: ingestion, preprocessing,\n * hyper-parameter tuning, model training, and registration.\n */\nstatic int start_training_pipeline(void)\n{\n    char tmp_data_dir[PATH_MAX]        = {0};\n    char preproc_output[PATH_MAX]      = {0};\n    char best_params_file[PATH_MAX]    = {0};\n    model_info_t model_info            = {0};\n\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Starting training pipeline…\");\n\n    /* 1. Ingest latest data */\n    if (lms_fetch_latest_data(g_orch.cfg.lms_endpoint,\n                              g_orch.cfg.project_root,\n                              tmp_data_dir,\n                              sizeof(tmp_data_dir)) != 0)\n    {\n        LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Data ingestion failed.\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* 2. Pre-processing & feature engineering */\n    if (pipeline_run(tmp_data_dir,\n                     preproc_output,\n                     sizeof(preproc_output)) != 0)\n    {\n        LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Pre-processing failed.\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* 3. Hyper-parameter tuning (optional) */\n    trainer_params_t params = {0};\n    if (g_orch.cfg.enable_hparam_tuning)\n    {\n        if (tuner_run(preproc_output,\n                      best_params_file,\n                      sizeof(best_params_file)) != 0)\n        {\n            LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Hyper-parameter tuning failed.\");\n            return -1;\n        }\n\n        if (params_load_from_file(best_params_file, &params) != 0)\n        {\n            LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Failed to parse best params file.\");\n            return -1;\n        }\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        params = trainer_default_params();\n    }\n\n    /* 4. Model training via Factory Pattern */\n    trainer_t *trainer = trainer_factory_create(g_orch.cfg.model_strategy);\n    if (!trainer)\n    {\n        LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Trainer creation failed (strategy=%s).\",\n                     g_orch.cfg.model_strategy);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    if (trainer->train(trainer, preproc_output, &params, &model_info) != 0)\n    {\n        LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Model training failed.\");\n        trainer->destroy(trainer);\n        return -1;\n    }\n    trainer->destroy(trainer);\n\n    /* 5. Register model in the central registry */\n    if (registry_register_model(&model_info) != 0)\n    {\n        LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Model registration failed.\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* 6. Notify Observers for model lifecycle event */\n    observer_event_t ev = {\n        .type      = OBSERVER_EVT_MODEL_VERSIONED,\n        .timestamp = time(NULL),\n        .payload   = &model_info\n    };\n    observer_broadcast(&ev);\n\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Training pipeline completed (model_id=%s).\",\n                model_info.model_id);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * scheduler_mainloop()\n * ----------------------------------------------------\n * Background thread that waits until the configured retrain\n * interval (or an external drift alert) elapses, then triggers\n * start_training_pipeline().\n */\nstatic void *scheduler_mainloop(void *arg)\n{\n    (void)arg; /* Unused */\n\n    const int interval_hours = g_orch.cfg.retrain_interval_hours > 0 ?\n                               g_orch.cfg.retrain_interval_hours :\n                               DEFAULT_RETRAIN_HRS;\n\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG,\n                \"Scheduler thread started (interval=%dh).\", interval_hours);\n\n    while (1)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&g_orch.state_mtx);\n        int keep_running = g_orch.running;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_orch.state_mtx);\n\n        if (!keep_running)\n            break;\n\n        /* Wait for either drift alert or time-based trigger */\n        scheduler_wait_or_timeout(interval_hours * 3600);\n\n        /* Consume any pending drift events */\n        if (observer_drift_detected())\n        {\n            LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG,\n                        \"Model drift detected – initiating immediate retraining.\");\n        }\n\n        if (!keep_running)\n            break;\n\n        if (start_training_pipeline() != 0)\n        {\n            LL_LOG_ERROR(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Scheduled training failed; will retry later.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Scheduler thread exiting.\");\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/**\n * handle_sigterm()\n * ----------------------------------------------------\n * Graceful shutdown handler for SIGINT/SIGTERM.\n */\nstatic void handle_sigterm(int signum)\n{\n    (void)signum; /* Unused */\n    LL_LOG_INFO(ORCHESTRATOR_TAG, \"Received termination signal. Shutting down…\");\n    orchestrator_stop();\n}\n```","numLines":320,"startLine":1,"totalLines":320}}}

*  lexilearn_orchestrator/src/controller/monitoring/model_monitor.h\n *\n *  Copyright (c) 2024 LexiLearn\n *\n *  Production-grade interface for Controller-level Model Monitoring.\n *  -----------------------------------------------------------------\n *  The Model Monitor is responsible for:\n *    • Persistently tracking model-level metrics (latency, accuracy, drift, etc.)\n *    • Emitting Observer-pattern events to trigger automated retraining\n *    • Acting as an abstraction layer so that multiple drift-detection\n *      strategies can be swapped in without touching higher-level code\n *    • Interfacing with the Model Registry to tag / version promoted models\n *\n *  This header intentionally contains no implementation logic—only the public\n *  contracts meant to be consumed by the Controller’s Pipeline components\n *  and, where relevant, by View-layer dashboards.\n */\n\n#ifndef LEXILEARN_CONTROLLER_MONITORING_MODEL_MONITOR_H\n#define Versioning                                  */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#define MODEL_MONITOR_VERSION_MAJOR  1\n#define MODEL_MONITOR_VERSION_MINOR  0\n#define MODEL_MONITOR_VERSION_PATCH  0\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Error Codes                                 */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    MM_OK = 0,                  /* Success */\n MM_ERR_STATE       = -4,    /* Monitor is in invalid state */\n    MM_ERR_INTERNAL    = -5,    /* Catch-all for unexpected errors */\n} mm_status_t;\n\n/* (a) : (b))\n   144→#define RT_MAX(a,b) ((a) > (b) ? (a) : (b))\n   145→\n   146→/* Return current time in milliseconds since epoch – for internal bookkeeping\n   147→ * when caller passes 0 for timestamp_ms.\n   148→ */\n   149→static uint64_t _rt_now_ms(void)\n   150→{\n   151→    struct timespec ts;\n   152→    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n   153→    return ((uint64_t) ts.tv_sec) * 1000ULL + (uint64_t) (ts.tv_nsec / 1000000ULL);\n   154→}\n   155→\n   156→/* Metric ring-buffer element */\n   157→typedef struct\n   158→{\n   159→    double   value;\n   160→    uint64_t ts_ms;\n   161→} rt_sample_t;\n   162→\n   163→/* Model registry entry */\n   164→typedef struct\n   165→{\n   166→    bool             in_use;\n   167→    char             model_id[RT_MODEL_ID_MAX];\n   168→\n   169→    /* configuration */\n   170→    rt_metric_t      metric_type;\n   171→    double           drift_threshold_pct;                 /* current number of samples */\n   177→    bool             retrain_pending;       /* drift detected, waiting for handler */\n   178→} rt_model_entry_t;\n   179→\n   180→/* Global registry */\n   181→static struct\n   182→{\n   183→    pthread_mutex_t      mtx;\n   184→    rt_model_entry_t     entries[RT_MAX_MODELS];\n   185→    rt_retrain_callback_t callback;\n   186→    bool                 initialized;\n   187→} _rt_state = {\n   188→    .mtx          = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER,\n   189→    .entries      = { {0} },\n   190→    .callback     = NULL,\n   191→    .initialized  = false\n   192→};\n   193→\n   194→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   195→ *  Internal Functions\n   196→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   197→\n   198→/* Thread-safe lookup (caller must hold lock) */\n   199→static rt_model_entry_t *_rt_find_entry(const char *model_id)\n   200→{\n   201→    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n   202→    {\n   203→        if (_rt_state.entries[i].in_use &&\n   204→            strncmp(_rt_state.entries[i].model_id, model_id, RT_MODEL_ID_MAX) == 0)\n   205→        {\n   206→            return &_rt_state.entries[i];\n   207→        }\n   208→    }\n   209→    return NULL;\n   210→}\n   211→\n   212→/* Compute average of N most recent samples from ring buffer */\n   213→static double _rt_avg_last_n(const rt_model_entry_t *ent, size_t n)\n   214→{\n   215→    if (ent->count == 0) { return 0.0; }\n   216→    n = RT_MIN(n, ent->count);\n   217→    double sum = 0.0;\n   218→    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\n   219→    {\n   220→        size_t idx = (ent->head + RT_MAX_SAMPLES - 1 - i) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   221→        sum += ent->samples[idx].value;\n   222→    }\n   223→    return sum / (double) n;\n   224→}\n   225→\n   226→/* Compute baseline average consisting of the oldest N samples */\n   227→static double _rt_avg_first_n(const rt_model_entry_t *ent, size_t n)\n   228→{\n   229→    if (ent->count == 0) { return 0.0; }\n   230→    n = RT_MIN(n, ent->count);\n   231→    double sum = 0.0;\n   232→    size_t start = (ent->head + RT_MAX_SAMPLES - ent->count) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   233→    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\n   234→    {\n   235→        size_t idx = (start + i) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   236→        sum += ent->samples[idx].value;\n   237→    }\n   238→    return sum / (double) n;\n   239→}\n   240→\n   241→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   242→ *  Public API – Implementation\n   243→ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   244→rt_status_t rt_trigger_init(rt_retrain_callback_t cb)\n   245→{\n   246→    if (cb == NULL) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   247→\n   248→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   249→    _rt_state.callback    = cb;\n   250→    _rt_state.initialized = true;\n   251→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   252→\n   253→    return RT_OK;\n   254→}\n   255→\n   256→void rt_trigger_shutdown(void)\n   257→{\n   258→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   259→    memset(_rt_state.entries, 0, sizeof(_rt_state.entries));\n   260→    _rt_state.callback    = NULL;\n   261→    _rt_state.initialized = false;\n   262→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   263→}\n   264→\n   265→rt_status_t rt_register_model(const char *model_id,\n   266→                              rt_metric_t metric,\n   267→                              double drift_threshold_pct)\n   268→{\n   269→    if (!model_id || drift_threshold_pct <= 0.0) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   270→\n   271→    rt_status_t status = RT_ERR_FULL;\n   272→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   273→\n   274→    /* duplicate? */\n   275→    if (_rt_find_entry(model_id) != NULL)\n   276→    {\n   277→        status = RT_ERR_ALREADY_EXISTS;\n   278→        goto out;\n   279→    }\n   280→\n   281→    /* locate free slot */\n   282→    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n   283→    {\n   284→        if (!_rt_state.entries[i].in_use)\n   285→        {\n   286→            rt_model_entry_t *ent = &_rt_state.entries[i];\n   287→            memset(ent, 0, sizeof(*ent));\n   288→            ent->in_use              = true;\n   289→            ent->metric_type         = metric;\n   290→            ent->drift_threshold_pct = drift_threshold_pct;\n   291→            strncpy(ent->model_id, model_id, RT_MODEL_ID_MAX - 1);\n   292→            status = RT_OK;\n   293→            goto out;\n   294→        }\n   295→    }\n   296→\n   297→out:\n   298→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   299→    return status;\n   300→}\n   301→\n   302→rt_status_t rt_unregister_model(const char *model_id)\n   303→{\n   304→    if (!model_id) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   305→\n   306→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   307→    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n   308→    if (ent)\n   309→    {\n   310→        memset(ent, 0, sizeof(*ent));\n   311→        pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   312→        return RT_OK;\n   313→    }\n   314→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   315→    return RT_ERR_NOT_FOUND;\n   316→}\n   317→\n   318→rt_status_t rt_record_metric(const char *model_id,\n   319→                             double      value,\n   320→                             uint64_t    timestamp_ms)\n   321→{\n   322→    if (!model_id) { return RT_ERR_BAD_PARAM; }\n   323→    if (timestamp_ms == 0) { timestamp_ms = _rt_now_ms(); }\n   324→\n   325→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   326→    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n   327→    if (!ent)\n   328→    {\n   329→        pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   330→        return RT_ERR_NOT_FOUND;\n   331→    }\n   332→\n   333→    /* insert into ring buffer */\n   334→    ent->samples[ent->head].value  = value;\n   335→    ent->samples[ent->head].ts_ms  = timestamp_ms;\n   336→    ent->head   = (ent->head + 1) % RT_MAX_SAMPLES;\n   337→    ent->count  = RT_MIN(ent->count + 1, RT_MAX_SAMPLES);\n   338→\n   339→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   340→    return RT_OK;\n   341→}\n   342→\n   343→/* Determine drift for a single entry (lock must be held) */\n   344→static bool _rt_drift_detected(rt_model_entry_t *ent)\n   345→{\n   346→    if (ent->count < RT_BASELINE_WINDOW * 2) { return false; }\n   347→\n   348→    double baseline = _rt_avg_first_n(ent, RT_BASELINE_WINDOW);\n   349→    double current  = _rt_avg_last_n(ent,  RT_BASELINE_WINDOW);\n   350→\n   351→    if (baseline == 0.0) { return false; } /* avoid div-by-zero */\n   352→\n   353→    double delta_pct;\n   354→    if (ent->metric_type == RT_METRIC_LOSS)\n   355→    {\n   356→        /* increase in loss indicates drift */\n   357→        delta_pct = ((current - baseline) / baseline) * 100.0;\n   358→        return delta_pct >= ent->drift_threshold_pct;\n   359→    }\n   360→    else\n   361→    {\n   362→        /* decrease in metric (accuracy, F1, etc.) indicates drift */\n   363→        delta_pct = ((baseline - current) / baseline) * 100.0;\n   364→        return delta_pct >= ent->drift_threshold_pct;\n   365→    }\n   366→}\n   367→\n   368→void rt_check_and_schedule(void)\n   369→{\n   370→    if (!_rt_state.initialized || !_rt_state.callback) { return; }\n   371→\n   372→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   373→\n   374→    /* scan registry */\n   375→    for (size_t i = 0; i < RT_MAX_MODELS; ++i)\n   376→    {\n   377→        rt_model_entry_t *ent = &_rt_state.entries[i];\n   378→        if (!ent->in_use || ent->retrain_pending) { continue; }\n   379→\n   380→        if (_rt_drift_detected(ent))\n   381→        {\n   382→            ent->retrain_pending = true;\n   383→            /* Unlock during callback to avoid deadlocks */\n   384→            pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   385→            bool ok = _rt_state.callback(ent->model_id);\n   386→            pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   387→\n   388→            if (ok)\n   389→            {\n   390→                /* Callback accepted the scheduling – reset samples to establish new baseline */\n   391→                ent->count            = 0;\n   392→                ent->head             = 0;\n   393→                ent->retrain_pending  = false;\n   394→            }\n   395→            /* else: keep pending flag set for future retries */\n   396→        }\n   397→    }\n   398→\n   399→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   400→}\n   401→\n   402→bool rt_has_pending_retraining(const char *model_id)\n   403→{\n   404→    if (!model_id) { return false; }\n   405→    bool pending = false;\n   406→    pthread_mutex_lock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   407→    rt_model_entry_t *ent = _rt_find_entry(model_id);\n   408→    if (ent) { pending = ent->retrain_pending; }\n   409→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&_rt_state.mtx);\n   410→    return pending;\n   411→}\n   412→\n   413→#endif /* LEXILEARN_RETRAINING_TRIGGER_IMPLEMENTATION */\n   414→#endif /* LEXILEARN_RETRAINING_TRIGGER_H */\n   415→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware.
